*comment -- WRITE ------------------------------------------
*comment -- Sets the variables to the corresponding array value.
*comment -- (a:number, b:number, c:number, d:number, e:number, f:number) -> (return_a:any?, return_b:any?, return_c:any?, return_d:any?, return_e:any?, return_f:any?)
*label write
*params a b c d e f

*set return_a (alpha6[a])
*set return_b (bravo6[b])
*set return_c (charlie6[c])
*set return_d (delta6[d])
*set return_e (echo6[e])
*set return_f (foxtrot6[f])

*return

*label next
*return
